- **gepa**: GEPA prompt-optimization trial operations — eval, history, invalidate, resume, revert, thaw. Consolidates the three prior gepa-eval/gepa-history/gepa-invalidate slash commands into one dispatcher; resume/revert/thaw were CLI-only (crew.mts) before this merge and had no slash-command wrapper. ([astragenie/dev-crew](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-crew/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa.md) · ★1)
- **gepa-eval**: Run a per-agent eval suite under a fileLockManager guard. Wraps `bun run evals` so concurrent runs for the same agent cannot corrupt the trial JSONL store. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-eval.md))
- **gepa-history**: Print recent GEPA trials for an agent (from .claude/artifacts/crew/gepa/trials/&lt;agent&gt;.jsonl) ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-history.md))
- **gepa-invalidate**: Soft-delete GEPA trials for an agent. Supports filtering by ISO datetime (--since) and tag (--tag). Writes an audit row to events.jsonl with invalidated_by_pid, invalidated_at, and reason. Invalidated trials are excluded from future recall() calls but preserved on disk (soft-delete semantics). ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-invalidate.md))
- **gepa-optimize**: Run one GEPA optimization cycle for an agent. Generates K candidate prompt variants, scores them, Pareto-ranks results, and writes an artifact JSON. When a winner is found and --artifact-only is not set, opens a promotion PR via gh CLI. Enforces daily budget cap and no-winner streak halt. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-optimize.md))
- **gepa-resume**: Clear a GEPA no-winner streak for a specific agent (re-enabling optimization after 3 consecutive no-winner cycles), OR clear the global optimize.paused flag in gepa.config.json. With an agent name: clears the streak. Without an agent name: clears the global pause. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-resume.md))
- **gepa-revert**: Revert a GEPA champion promotion. Deletes the soak pointer for the agent from soak.json and, if a promotion commit exists on main, runs `git revert &lt;commit&gt;` (never git reset, never git push --force). Reads prior_prompt_hash from gepa frontmatter to locate the commit. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-revert.md))
- **gepa-thaw**: Remove an agent from the gepa.config.json champion_frozen list, re-enabling optimization and promotion cycles for that agent. Uses atomic tmp+rename write to prevent partial updates. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-thaw.md))
